### [1] [Dear Quantizers, Stochastic $\approx$ Brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38209)
*Justin Kulp*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将随机量子化解释为通过一个(d+1)维超对称Langevin系统实现d维欧氏量子场论的平衡极限，并借助Morse理论与上同调拓扑场论（EqmTFT）统一刻画其BV/BRST结构及解析延拓下的几何极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立随机量子化与拓扑场论、几何分析（如Morse理论）之间的深层联系，为非平衡统计场论与量子化方法提供统一的数学框架。

Method: 采用随机系统的超对称形式化，结合Morse理论分析Langevin动力学；引入共形不变的平衡拓扑场论（EqmTFT）作为'绝对化'工具，并利用解析延拓研究A模型及Carrollian/非相对论极限。

Result: 证明随机量子化可视为相对QFT经EqmTFT‘绝对化’的过程；揭示其Neumann型边界条件等价于d维BV/BRST复形；发现解析延拓下与A模型、非相对论及Carrollian极限存在系统性对应。

Conclusion: 随机量子化不仅是计算工具，更是连接欧氏QFT、拓扑场论、几何分析与对称性破缺极限的结构性桥梁；EqmTFT提供了协变且上同调精确的平衡态描述。

Abstract: Stochastic quantization formulates $d$-dimensional Euclidean QFT as the equilibrium limit of a $(d+1)$-dimensional Langevin system. Using the supersymmetric formulation of stochastic systems, we apply techniques from Morse theory to study Langevin dynamics. We interpret stochastic quantization as the absolutization of a relative QFT by a bulk cohomological topological Equilibrium TFT ("EqmTFT"), and relate its enriched Neumann boundary data to the $d$-dimensional BV/BRST complex. Analytic continuation reveals connections to the A-model and non-relativistic and Carrollian limits.

</details>


### [2] [Construction of mirror pairs of Berglund-Hubsch Calabi-Yau orbifolds of loop-type polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38273)
*Maxim Malyutin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了一种构造Berglund-Hubsch-Krawitz型Calabi-Yau流形及其镜像对的orbifold算法，针对loop型多项式；完全枚举出216个满足Calabi-Yau条件的loop多项式，计算其Euler特征数、代数代数数与singlet数，并利用Roan广义组合构造分析twisted sector态；发现所有构型均无Roan对，即twisted sector对Hodge数差在orbifold奇点消解后无贡献；关键物理结果是找到两个具有三代夸克-轻子（N_gen=3）的构型，指数为{74,3,3,5,3}和{74,3,5,3,3}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有三代费米子的phenomenologically realistic Calabi-Yau compactification模型，检验orbifold镜像对称性在loop型BHK构型中的实现，并系统研究twisted sector对Hodge结构的影响。

Method: 基于Berglund-Hubsch-Krawitz构造，对loop型加权齐次多项式实施完整枚举；运用Roan's generalized combinatorial construction分析orbifold twisted sector；通过Lefschetz不动点定理与Poincaré对偶计算Euler特征数及Hodge数；显式计算orbifold群作用下的generations（h^{2,1}）与singlets（h^{1,1}）。

Result: 获得216个Calabi-Yau loop多项式；确认所有构型中Roan pairs不存在，故twisted sector不改变h^{1,1}−h^{2,1}；发现两例精确给出N_gen = h^{2,1} = 3的构型：指数向量{74,3,3,5,3}与{74,3,5,3,3}。

Conclusion: Loop型BHK orbifolds中twisted sector对Hodge差无净贡献；存在离散参数空间中的孤立点支持标准模型三代结构，为F-theory或heterotic string compactification提供了新的几何候选。

Abstract: In this paper, an algorithm for constructing orbifolds of Calabi-Yau manifolds of the Berglund-Hubsch-Krawitz (BHK) type and their mirror pairs for loop-type polynomials is proposed and applied. A complete enumeration of loop polynomials satisfying the Calabi-Yau condition was carried out, yielding 216 unique polynomials. For each polynomial, the Euler characteristics, the numbers of generations and singlets were computed, and the twisted sector states were analyzed using Roan's generalized combinatorial construction. It was established that Roan pairs are absent for all studied configurations, indicating a zero contribution of the twisted sectors to the difference in Hodge numbers upon resolving the orbifold singularities. The primary physical result of this work is the discovery of two configurations with diagonal exponents $\{74, 3, 3, 5, 3\}$ and $\{74, 3, 5, 3, 3\}$, for which the number of quark and lepton generations is precisely three ($N_{\text{gen}} = 3$), which is of key interest for constructing phenomenologically realistic models of particle physics.

</details>


### [3] [Detectors in Nonrelativistic Conformal Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38301)
*Cyuan-Han Chang,Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Ian Moult,Dam Thanh Son*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了非相对论共形场论（NRCFT）中的速度微分能量流探测算符 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$，给出其非微扰定义、对称性与代数结构；计算了其在自由费米子、幺正费米气体及Efimov三体态等典型态中的一点函数，揭示出共振相互作用诱导的‘喷注’结构；并据此导出非相对论版本的Hofman-Maldacena共形对撞机约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受相对论QFT中探测算符成功形式化渐近通量实验的启发，亟需将该框架推广至非相对论共形场论（NRCFT），以刻画冷原子、低能中子等实验中同时依赖角度与速度分布的通量测量。

Method: 基于NRCFT的Schrödinger对称性构造速度微分能量流算符 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$，建立其非微扰定义；利用共形不变性与守恒律分析其变换性质与算符代数；在自由费米子、幺正费米气体及Efimov三体态中显式计算其真空与热/少体态的一点函数；通过要求 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$ 在自旋1态中正定，推导非相对论共形对撞机不等式。

Result: 1) $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$ 具有明确的Schrödinger协变性，其代数封闭于扩展的共形流代数；2) 在Efimov三体态中，一阶通量呈现周期性振荡与速度空间局域增强的‘喷注’结构；3) 导出首个非相对论Hofman-Maldacena型约束：$\langle \mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n}) \rangle \geq 0$ 在所有自旋1共形初态下成立；4) 提出冷原子动量分辨射线照相与中子速度选择谱仪为可行实验实现路径。

Conclusion: 速度微分能量流探测算符 $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$ 是NRCFT中连接共形对称性、少体动力学与实验可观测量的核心工具；其在Efimov物理与共形不等式中的应用，标志着非相对论共形探测理论的实质性开端。

Abstract: Correlations in asymptotic fluxes provide one of the primary experimental means of studying physical systems. In the context of relativistic QFT, the formalization of such experiments in terms of detector operators has led to significant progress. Motivated by these successes, in this paper we initiate a study of detector operators in nonrelativistic conformal field theories (NRCFTs). In NRCFTs, fluxes are distributed in both angle and velocity, making the natural detector operator the energy flux differential in velocity, $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$. We provide non-perturbative definitions of these operators in NRCFTs, and study their symmetries and algebra. We calculate one-point functions of these detector observables in a variety of few and many body states of representative NRCFTs, including free fermions, as well as fermions at unitarity. In interacting three-body states, they exhibit a remarkably rich behavior reflective of the Efimov three-body wave-function, including the formation of an enhanced "jet" structure due to the presence of resonant interactions. We use these detectors to derive a nonrelativistic generalization of the Hofman-Maldacena conformal collider bounds by arguing for positivity of $\mathcal{E}_v(\hat{n})$, and imposing this in spin 1 states. We outline a number of future directions for the study of detectors in NRCFTs, and discuss how they can be measured in a variety of experimental platforms from cold atoms to low energy neutron experiments.

</details>

*Nikolay Bobev,Pieter Bomans,Fridrik Freyr Gautason*

TL;DR: 本文研究了Dp-膜反作用导致的大范围尺度协变弦背景，通过Weyl共形变换使度规含AdS因子、胀子具非平凡轮廓，从而建立非共形强耦合规范理论的全息对偶；构造了多类新型超对称背景（含D2-膜解、紧致化包裹解及十维无穷族），并计算了全息RG流与低能算符谱，预言了热自由能和标量两点函数等可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建非共形强耦合规范理论的可控全息对偶框架，弥补AdS/CFT在非共形情形下的缺失，探索尺度协变性在弦论背景中的实现及其场论含义。

Method: 采用IIB/IIA超引力理论，在特定Weyl共形帧下求解Dp-膜背反应背景；运用四维截断超引力、Dp-膜在紧致流形上的包裹构造、以及直接十维解析构造法；结合全息重整化群流分析与线性化扰动理论计算算符谱和关联函数。

Result: 发现若干新型超对称D2-膜背景（含四维gauged supergravity导出解、包裹解及十维无穷族）；构造了它们之间的全息RG流；计算了低能局域算符谱；确定了热自由能F ~ T^{3/2}与标量两点函数⟨𝒪𝒪⟩ ∼ k^{Δ−d}的全息形式。

Conclusion: 尺度协变弦背景为非共形规范理论提供了严格全息实现；新解族揭示了超对称破缺模式与IR动力学多样性；预言的热力学与关联函数可被格点规范理论检验，强化了AdS/dCFT范式的物理可靠性。

Abstract: We explore a vast landscape of scale-covariant string backgrounds that arise from the backreaction of D$p$-branes. In a convenient Weyl frame the metric has an AdS factor and the dilaton has a non-trivial profile. This structure provides a notion of scale covariance that facilitates a holographic analysis and provides a window into the dynamics of the dual strongly coupled non-conformal gauge theory. We present different classes of supergravity solutions of this kind and discuss their dual field theory interpretation. We find several new supersymmetric backgrounds arising from D2-branes by utilizing four-dimensional gauged supergravity, D$p$-branes wrapped on compact manifolds, and infinite families constructed directly in ten dimensions. We construct holographic RG flows between some of the new supersymmetric backgrounds and compute the spectrum of low-lying local operators. Holography determines the form of observables such as the thermal free energy and scalar two-point functions, predictions which could be tested on the lattice.

</details>


### [5] [Fractional Superfluid Vortices and Worldsheet Wess--Zumino--Witten Models in Two-Color QCD and $Sp(2N)$ Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39760)
*Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了双色QCD及更一般的偶数味数N_f=2m的赝实规范理论在有限重子密度下的超流涡旋，发现存在分数最小涡旋（baryon-phase winding ν=1/m），其核心为Sp(2)≈SU(2)；当涡旋嵌入正交味平面时，手征作用量精确可分解；味间零模在涡旋重合时局域化，使对称性从[Sp(2)]^m增强至Sp(2m)；并推导出涡旋上携带的Sp(2n)_N与Sp(2m)_{Nν} WZW理论，建立非超对称四维/二维对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解赝实规范理论（如2-color QCD）在高密度下超流相中拓扑缺陷（特别是分数涡旋）的量子化结构及其低能有效理论，探索四维体理论与二维涡旋世界面理论之间的对偶性。

Method: 基于反称二夸克凝聚态的拓扑分类，结合手征有效理论、零模分析、Wess-Zumino-Witten项的bulk-to-vortex descent机制，以及Sp(2N)规范群下基本费米子的全局对称性提升论证。

Result: 1) 发现baryon-phase winding ν=1/m的分数最小涡旋，核心为Sp(2)；2) 正交味嵌入下手征作用量精确因子化；3) 涡旋重合导致味零模局域化，对称性增强至Sp(2m)；4) 单位重子绕数涡旋携带Sp(2m)_N WZW理论，中心荷c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)；5) 提出非超对称4D/2D对应框架。

Conclusion: 赝实规范理论中超流涡旋承载受控的非阿贝尔WZW理论，其对称性与中心荷由味数、颜色结构及涡旋叠加方式共同决定，为强耦合QCD类系统提供了严格可处理的拓扑激发范例，并暗示了一类普适的体-边界对应关系。

Abstract: We study superfluid vortices in two-color QCD and more general pseudoreal gauge theories with even flavor number $N_f=2m$ at finite baryon density. The antisymmetric diquark condensate admits fractional minimal vortices with baryon-phase winding $ν=1/m$, corresponding to $1/m$ of the conventional circulation quantum, each localizing an $Sp(2)\simeq SU(2)$ core sector. For vortices embedded in mutually orthogonal flavor planes, the leading-order chiral action factorizes exactly. Off-diagonal flavor zero modes are non-normalizable at finite separation but become localized at coincidence, enhancing the normalizable sector from $[Sp(2)]^m$ to $Sp(2m)$. For fundamental fermions of an $Sp(2N)$ gauge theory, the bulk Wess--Zumino--Witten (WZW) term descends compatibly: a cluster of $n$ coincident minimal vortices carries an $Sp(2n)_N$ WZW theory, while a flavor-symmetric vortex with positive integer baryon-phase winding $ν$ carries $Sp(2m)_{Nν}$. At unit winding this yields the vortex map to $Sp(2m)_N$, with central charge $c=Nm(2m+1)/(N+m+1)$ at the WZW fixed point, and suggests a broader nonsupersymmetric four-dimensional/two-dimensional correspondence.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分仅由超引力本身导出的性质与需依赖一致量子引力紫外完成的性质，检验Swampland猜想在N=2超引力中的普适性。

Result: （1）无穷远退化类型与五/六维去紧化或弱耦合弦极限在规范耦合层面精确匹配；（2）存在Kähler基使预势三重耦合非负；（3）提出两个量子引力相容性判据：Hodge结构整性与简单正规交叉紧化存在性。

Conclusion: N=2超引力的无穷远模空间结构蕴含深层量子引力约束，这些约束不依赖具体UV实现，表明Swampland原理具有普适几何根源。

Abstract: We study the behaviour of general four-dimensional N=2 supergravity theories near boundaries at infinity of the vector multiplet moduli space, with a focus on a clear distinction between properties that follow from supergravity alone rather than from a consistent UV completion in quantum gravity. The basis for our analysis is the appearance of a real variation of polarised Hodge structure governing the vector multiplet moduli space of N=2 supergravity, combined with very recent advances in the mathematics literature. The resulting classification of infinite distance degenerations exactly agrees, at the level of gauge couplings, with a possible interpretation of the limits as decompactification limits to five or six dimensions or as weakly coupled string limits, as expected from a quantum gravity perspective. This observation extends previous results found in the context of string compactifications on Calabi--Yau varieties and, remarkably, holds for general four-dimensional N=2 supergravity theories regardless of a UV completion. We furthermore show the existence of a Kähler basis in which the triple couplings of the prepotential are non-negative, irrespective of a geometric origin of the supergravity theory. Finally we propose two non-trivial constraints for a four-dimensional N=2 supergravity to be compatible with quantum gravity: Integrality of the Hodge structure as a necessary condition for the completeness hypothesis and the existence of a simple normal crossing compactification of the moduli space to ensure compatibility of the weakly coupled asymptotic gravitational duality frames.

</details>

Result: 1) 建立了伪荷守恒恒等式作为弱破缺规范理论的bootstrap约束；2) 在AdS Yang-Mills中确定了混合量子化下两扇区耦合的约束；3) 在双AdS引力Higgs模型中，伪荷恒等式在任意破缺参数下成立，而普通荷恒等式仅在无耦合极限下恢复因子化；4) 在dS共形引力中，成功约束了graviton与部分质量自旋-2场的相互作用顶点，结果与已知bulk理论一致。

Conclusion: 伪荷守恒恒等式构成一类普适的、非局域的CFT一致性条件，可系统刻画de Sitter与anti-de Sitter背景下弱破缺规范/共形对称性及其对应的bulk质量生成机制，为高能物理中对称性破缺与全息bootstrap的交叉研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: We develop a bootstrap approach for weakly broken gauge theories in (anti-)de Sitter space, focusing on cases in which the conservation of the dual boundary current is broken by a double-trace operator. Integrating the corresponding Ward identity, we derive pseudo-charge conservation identities that contain fixed nonlocal contributions as integrals of three-point functions. These identities impose consistency conditions on the space of allowed theories. As an illustrative example, we study Yang-Mills theory in AdS with symmetry-breaking boundary conditions for charged matter and show that the pseudo-charge conservation identities constrain the mixing between the two quantization sectors. We then apply the framework to the Higgs mechanism for gravity in two AdS spaces with a common boundary, coupled so that the corresponding stress tensors are not separately conserved. In this setting, the ordinary charge conservation identities recover the factorization into two independent CFTs, whereas the pseudo-charge conservation identities hold for arbitrary values of the symmetry-breaking parameter. Finally, for conformal gravity in de Sitter space, we constrain the interactions between the graviton and the partially massless spin-2 field, both in the minimal theory and in the presence of additional scalar or vector matter, finding agreement with the predictions of the corresponding bulk theories.

</details>

*Peng-Yu Wu,H. Lu*

Abstract: Charged particle track reconstruction at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider is challenged by dense hit environments and combinatorial ambiguities. Across five simulation benchmarks spanning linear, helical, and reduced TrackML events with up to 200--500 simultaneous trajectories, EGT achieves the highest FitAccuracy on three of five settings under the shared benchmark protocol, reaching 79.6\% on the most complex setting and exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.6 percentage points. Under synthetic background injection equal to the number of signal hits, FitAccuracy decreases by 3.4 percentage points. The neural-network forward pass requires 31~ms per event on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. Abstract: In atmospheric neutrino analyses, the usable zenith-angle range is generally restricted to upward-going events to suppress the cosmic-ray muon background. Motivated by the exceptionally low underground muon background at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL), we investigate whether extending the zenith-angle acceptance can improve the sensitivity of atmospheric neutrino oscillation measurements. An existing 1725 m$^{3}$ liquid-nitrogen volume is adopted as the basis of a simplified detector model, in which the accepted zenith-angle range is extended from $\cosθ_μ\in[-1,0]$ to $\cosθ_μ\in[-1,0.3]$, and the projected oscillation sensitivity is evaluated for a 10-year exposure. The atmospheric neutrino flux is estimated by interpolating standard flux predictions as a function of geomagnetic latitude. Neutrino interactions in the surrounding rock and the liquid-nitrogen volume are simulated for neutrino energies of $E\ge0.1$ GeV, followed by secondary-particle transport and geometry-based event selection. For the benchmark oscillation parameters $Δ$m$^{2}_{32}$ = $2.4\times10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}^{2}$ and $\sin^{2}θ_{23}=0.5$, the neutrino-induced muon flux from the surrounding rock is estimated to be $(3.65 \pm 1.00)\times10^{-13}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$, corresponding to a muon yield of $(0.13 \pm 0.034)$ day$^{-1}$ in the 1725 m$^{3}$ liquid-nitrogen volume. Three-flavor neutrino oscillations are incorporated into a Poisson-likelihood $χ^{2}$ analysis to evaluate the sensitivity to the oscillation parameters. Extending the usable zenith-angle range leads to a clear improvement in the projected oscillation sensitivity at the 90\% confidence level over a 10-year exposure.

Abstract: A measurement is presented of the triple-differential production cross section of Z/$γ^*$($\to$ $μμ$)+jet events using proton-proton collision data recorded at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC. The data, collected in the years 2016$-$2018, correspond to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The cross section is measured as a function of the transverse momentum of the muon pair, the boost in rapidity of the center-of-mass system of the muon pair and the leading jet with respect to the laboratory frame, and half of their absolute rapidity separation. This choice of observables ensures sensitivity to the scattering angle in the center-of-mass frame and the fractional momenta of the interacting partons. After unfolding for detector effects in all three dimensions simultaneously, the results are compared with predictions at next-to-next-to-leading order in perturbative quantum chromodynamics corrected for electroweak and nonperturbative effects. This measurement provides valuable information towards characterizing the partonic structure of the proton and determining the strong coupling.

</details>

Abstract: A precise measurement of prompt and non-prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$-meson spin alignment with respect to the helicity and production axes in proton-proton (pp) collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV is presented, using data collected with the ALICE experiment at the LHC. The spin alignment is quantified by measuring the diagonal spin density matrix element $ρ_{\rm 00}$ for ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons at midrapidity ($|y|<0.8$). The measurement is performed in the transverse-momentum ($p_{\rm T}$) range $3<p_{\rm T}<100$ GeV/$c$ for prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons and $3<p_{\rm T}<30$ GeV/$c$ for non-prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons, respectively. The measured $ρ_{\rm 00}$ values for prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons are consistent with the unpolarised expectation of $1/3$ over the entire $p_{\rm T}$ range, indicating no evidence of spin alignment. In contrast, $ρ_{\rm 00}$ of non-prompt ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ mesons exhibit a deviation from $1/3$, consistent with expectations from weak beauty-hadron decays and well described by PYTHIA 8 simulations coupled with the EvtGen decay package. These results confirm and extend previous measurements, providing a precise baseline for future studies of ${\rm D}^{\rm *+}$ spin alignment in heavy-ion collisions.

Abstract: A novel approach to extract decay amplitudes in $B\to V(\to M_1M_2)\ell^+\ell^-$ processes, where $V$ represents a meson with either $J = 0$ (S-wave) or $J = 1$ (P-wave), was recently proposed. In this method, the dependence of the amplitudes on the dihadron and dimuon invariant masses is extracted model-independently through a fit to the helicity angles and a subsequent calculation of sPlot weights. In this paper, we show that this method still performs well when applied to data with experimental realism, namely in the presence of detection efficiencies and background candidates, both of which can have non-factorising dependencies between the fitted helicity angles and extracted two-particle masses. We show that a non-factorising efficiency can be accommodated with a small change to the conventional calculation of the sPlot weights, and a non-factorising background by expanding the description of the background distribution into individually factorising terms.
  We illustrate the method using simulated $B\to K^+π^-μ^+μ^-$ data, containing both S- and P-wave contributions to the $K^+π^-$ system, mixed with non-factorising background candidates and generated with a realistic function for the detection efficiency. We also show that this method can be applied over a much larger dimuon invariant-mass range than studied previously. This work suggests that the method is suitable for application to real experimental data, where it could provide model-independent decay-amplitude shapes for direct comparison with theoretical predictions. A measurement using this technique can improve sensitivity to potential new-physics effects in $b\to s\ell\ell$ transitions, as well as the understanding of hadronic form factors, particularly in the S-wave system.

Abstract: We present the first double-differential cross-section measurements of muon neutrino charged-current interactions on argon with no mesons and one or more protons ($N_{p} \geq 1$) in the final state as a function of proton multiplicity and kinematics. The analysis uses the full Booster Neutrino Beam dataset collected by the MicroBooNE detector at Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory, corresponding to a total of $1.30 \times 10^{21}$ protons on target. We measure cross sections as a function of the proton multiplicity, the momenta and muon-proton opening angles of the leading and subleading protons up to and including the fourth-leading proton, as well as the total available kinetic energy of the hadronic system. The treatment of the statistical and systematic uncertainties includes correlations between the different extracted distributions. The measured cross sections are compared to predictions from several event generators using different primary and final-state interaction models. The results reveal strong sensitivity to the modeling of final-state interactions, and none of the generators tested provides a fully adequate description of the data. Significant disagreement between data and predictions is observed in the proton multiplicity distributions, particularly at backward opening angles and for subleading proton kinematics.

### [72] [Climate, Science, and the FCC: Think Clearly, Choose Wisely, Act Swiftly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30180)

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向公众的政策与科学伦理讨论文章，旨在回应针对CERN未来环形对撞机（FCC）的气候与社会批评，通过量化分析、全生命周期评估与替代方案探讨，主张不能仅以碳足迹否定基础科学设施，而应将其置于社会技术转型的整体框架中审慎权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应法国物理学会粒子与场分会及公众对FCC项目在气候责任、资源分配与科学必要性方面的强烈质疑，澄清误解并提供基于实证与伦理的综合判断框架。

Method: 采用跨学科论证方法：结合高能物理实验规划、碳足迹生命周期评估（LCA）、能源系统建模（含天然氢潜力）、机会成本分析及科技民主化公共咨询实践（CNDP与瑞士磋商）。

Result: 证明FCC的直接碳排放远低于其科学产出的社会价值；指出‘放弃大科学’并非气候解决方案，反而可能削弱清洁能源基础研究能力；提出通过模块化建设、绿电采购与天然氢耦合可显著压低净环境影响。

Conclusion: FCC的命运不应由单一气候指标决定，而需在‘收缩型社会’与‘转型型创新社会’之间做出根本性价值选择；基础科学基础设施是可持续未来不可或缺的认知与技术杠杆。

Abstract: Could a collider become the symbol of everything that must be abandoned to save the climate? In the summer of 2026, this question ran through discussions within the Particles and Fields Division of the French Physical Society. An unacceptable carbon footprint, two EPR nuclear reactors, five hundred deaths, billions to be reallocated and, to top it all, unclear scientific objectives: the objections to CERN's Future Circular Collider (FCC) appeared to form an overwhelming indictment. This essay examines each charge in turn. Grounded in a clear and compelling scientific vision, it confronts emotionally charged imagery with orders of magnitude, distinguishes a project's footprint from its full balance sheet, considers the costs of postponing or abandoning it, and explores ways of reducing its impacts-including the still uncertain prospect of natural hydrogen. The climate alone does not decide the future of the FCC; it requires us to judge the available choices by their real consequences. Developed for the public debate organised by France's National Commission for Public Debate (CNDP) and for the Swiss consultation, this essay is intended for the broadest possible readership, well beyond particle physics and the scientific community. It frames the FCC debate as a choice about the kind of society we want to build: should we organise society around renunciation and ever-shrinking horizons, or transform how we produce and choose a future worth wanting?

</details>

Abstract: Neutrino plasmas support flavor waves, collective oscillations of flavor, that neutrinos can resonantly emit or absorb when they fulfill the Cherenkov condition. Abrupt mode termination, instead, persists generically, except for special energy distributions which are entire functions. A main difference between the two interpretations is that one expects small cross sections and large flux rates for WIMPs, whereas it is the opposite for supermassive gravitinos, namely large (electromagnetic) cross sections and very low flux rates. The observed nuclear recoil energy of about 250 keV and the occurrence of one event in one year fit well with the estimates quoted in (K.A. Meissner, H.~Nicolai, Eur.Phys.J.C 84 (2024) 3, 269). A main characteristic of the new interpretation distinguishing it unequivocally from the WIMP hypothesis would be the sequential occurrence of two or more recoils within a few microseconds along the gravitino's straight trajectory, a prediction that remains to be checked. Such a measurement would also allow to overcome the `neutrino floor'. In this note we refine our previous estimates and propose some extra steps so as to enable the experiment to discriminate between the WIMP and gravitino hypotheses.

Abstract: I investigate the threshold double logarithmic behavior of the fragmentation function of a gluon into $^1S_0^{[1,8]}$ quarkonia, which is suppressed by $1/N$ in Mellin space. While threshold double logarithms have been resummed to all orders in the strong coupling for gluon fragmentation functions for quarkonia at leading power in $1/N$, it has not been known how to resum them for channels suppressed by powers of $1/N$. Threshold resummation can be important for the $^1S_0^{[1,8]}$ channels, as the tree-level gluon fragmentation functions have finite yet nonzero values at the kinematical threshold. I derive a formalism for resumming the threshold double logarithms for these channels at the leading double logarithmic level and discuss the phenomenological impact.

</details>

Abstract: Extracting the pion electromagnetic form factor $F_π(s)$ through phenomenological curve-fitting models introduces model dependence, unphysical artefacts, and kinematic inconsistencies. We introduce a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) embedded in a conformal $z$-plane that constructs $F_π(s)$ directly from first principles across spacelike and timelike domains: charge normalisation and Schwarz reflection are enforced by construction, while Cauchy-Riemann analyticity, dispersion relations, Watson's theorem, and perturbative QCD asymptotics enter through the loss functional. Thus, the fundamental S-matrix principles dictate the form factor's behaviour while data act as constraints. Mapping the cut complex plane onto the unit disk bounds the Hessian norm and prevents Neural Tangent Kernel spectral starvation, two known failure modes of deep-learning optimisation. Besides $e^+e^-$ scattering data, we also incorporate $τ$-decay data through a switch that isolates the pure isovector form factor natively, bypassing model-dependent isospin-breaking pre-corrections. The network organically yields an interior zero-free form factor, while the framework tests experimental tensions around the $ρ(770)$ peak against analyticity and dispersion constraints. Remarkably, for a given ratio $m_Q/Λ_d$, these observations can be used to infer the value of $N$. The resulting self-interaction phenomenology favors GeV-scale dark baryons, independently of the relic-abundance requirement. At these masses, a dark baryon asymmetry comparable to the visible one can account for the observed dark-matter abundance.

### [87] [Production Shape Effects Beyond Rate Rescaling in Dark Photon Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40052)

TL;DR: 本文研究长寿命粒子搜寻中产生模型不确定性对探测器接受度的影响，重点考察运动学分布效应而非总产额；通过比较修正Weizsäcker–Williams描述与有效准实近似（QRA）在归一化暗光子产生谱下的接受度差异，发现NA62实验中存在显著的非因子化效应（δ_NF > 0.3），且该效应不随QRA离壳尺度或孔径变化而消除；同时，公开的NA62全选接受度数据显示Mixing与Bremsstrahlung产生机制的dimuon接受度比值非恒定（0.58–4.23），中位绝对偏差达0.79，进一步证实接受度依赖于产生机制；文章提出‘投影判据’作为因子化是否成立的定量检验标准，并强调当不满足时，必须将产生模型依赖性传播进接受度计算或作为系统误差处理。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命粒子（LLP）搜寻中，理论产生模型的不确定性不仅影响总截面，更可能通过改变运动学分布而系统性偏移探测器接受度，从而引入未被充分评估的系统误差；现有分析常隐含‘产率因子化’假设（即接受度仅依赖于物理量而与产生机制无关），但该假设缺乏严格验证。

Conclusion: 产率因子化在NA62暗光子搜寻中明显失效；接受度不可视为与产生机制无关的普适函数；必须将产生模型依赖性显式传播至接受度计算中，或将其作为不可忽略的接受度系统不确定性予以量化和处理；投影判据为未来LLP分析提供了模型依赖性评估的通用框架。

Abstract: Production model uncertainties in long-lived particle searches can affect both the inclusive yield and the kinematic distributions sampled by the experimental acceptance. We isolate the latter effect by comparing the modified Weizsäcker--Williams description with the effective quasireal approximation for dark photon production via proton bremsstrahlung after normalizing both spectra on a common physical domain. At fixed production yield within this domain, the resulting acceptance difference directly measures the departure from rate factorization. In the NA62 beam dump geometry, the central calculation gives a fractional acceptance shift $δ_{\rm NF}>0.3$ at every numerically converged grid point within the displayed NA62 sensitivity overlap. This conclusion persists for effective QRA off-shell scales of 1.0, 1.5, and 2.0~GeV and under the aperture variations considered. Separately, the public NA62 full selection acceptances give a nonconstant dimuon acceptance ratio between the Mixing and Bremsstrahlung production categories, ranging from approximately 0.58 to 4.23, with a median absolute departure from unity of approximately 0.79 over the analyzed HEPData grid. This provides complementary evidence that the published full selection acceptance depends on the production category. More generally, the projection criterion provides a quantitative test of rate factorization. The normalized production shape variation must have negligible projection onto the acceptance response at the required accuracy. When this condition is not satisfied, production model dependence must be propagated through the acceptance calculation or treated as an acceptance uncertainty.

</details>

Abstract: The discovery of the Higgs boson cemented the Standard Model of particle physics (SM) as we know it and observations have so far confirmed SM predictions. Now, too many beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) models can be explored to overcome the remaining shortcomings of the SM. As a result, generic models present a useful intermediary step to point towards the next extension of the SM probing multiple Ultraviolet models simultaneously. In this work, two examples are investigated. First, off-shell effects of potential heavy new particles are provided in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) through higher dimensional operators. In particular, Charge-parity (CP) symmetry violating contributions from Wilson coefficients are ranked by using U(1) global flavour symmetries on CP-odd operators of the Warsaw basis. Asymmetries of simple triple products are proven to be more sensitive than the cross-section to these SMEFT operators and more efficient than most of the usual observables used in experiments. Then, on-shell effects of singlet and octet scalars of SUC(3) are independently searched for in four-top production. The signal consists of the associated production of a scalar with a top quark pair and of pair production in the case of the octet producing boosted top-quark pairs. The processes are generated at next-to-leading order in quantum chromodynamics with a careful renormalisation procedure and the correct selection of loop diagrams. Using improvements in top-jet tagging techniques and in the main background simulation as well as a new technique to select the events, a new particle search is performed to constrain the respective mass and coupling to top quarks of new resonances.

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R\simeq248$ keV. We study two supersymmetric interpretations based on distinct neutralino couplings to the $Z$ boson. In the elastic case, a bino- or singlino-like neutralino scatters through a diagonal axial $Z$ coupling set by the Higgsino asymmetry $X=|N_{13}|^2-|N_{14}|^2$ rather than by the total Higgsino fraction. Since the spin-dependent blind spot does not coincide with the spin-independent one, Higgs-mediated scattering can be suppressed while retaining $X\sim10^{-2}$. In the MSSM this favors low $\tanβ$, in tension with the observed Higgs mass for a few-TeV squark spectrum, a tension relieved by the tree-level quartic of the NMSSM. An explicit NMSSM benchmark with $m_χ\simeq500$ GeV and $X\simeq0.007$, below current LZ limits, obtains its relic abundance from a singlet-like pseudoscalar resonance just above threshold, which also suppresses present-day annihilation. In the inelastic case, a pseudo-Dirac Higgsino scatters endothermically, with a splitting of a few hundred keV generated by gauginos at the PeV scale which can be lowered by opposite-sign bino-wino cancellation, or by weak singlino mixing; the minimal thermal realization is strongly constrained by solar-neutrino searches. The two mechanisms predict distinct recoil spectra away from the candidate.

Abstract: We compare three approaches for computing color-summed tree-level all-gluon squared matrix elements: evaluation using direct contraction in the trace and adjoint decompositions, orthonormal multiplet bases, and fast Fourier transforms (FFT) based on the irreducible representations of the symmetric group for the trace and adjoint decompositions. The multiplet method reduces the color sum to a sum of absolute squares and utilizes an amplitude recursion labeled by SU(3) representations. The FFT exploits the relative-permutation dependence of color overlaps to replace the direct double sum with smaller independent contractions, using standard color-ordered partial amplitudes. This invokes the symmetric group at the level of gluon labels, and therefore makes maximal use of the permutation symmetry. We compare CPU time and memory use for four through eleven gluons. At eleven gluons, the direct adjoint contraction evaluates a fixed-helicity color-summed matrix element in an estimated 1.7 10${}^3$ s after initialization, compared with 19.8 s for the multiplet recursion and 0.814 s for the FFT adjoint method. The adjoint FFT is fastest from six through eleven gluons, despite its factorial scaling compared with the exponential scaling of the multiplet recursion.

Factorization breaking is incorporated through a $p_T$-dependent rapidity-gap survival probability calculated with the dynamic multiparton-interaction model of Pythia8. With the non-perturbative parameters profiled, the nominal relative shifts in $α_s$ are -0.0072%, -0.043%, -0.070%, and -0.067% at 5, 7, 8, and 13 TeV, respectively. In the W-mass study, the largest bias from an unmodelled survived single-diffractive contribution is 1.58 MeV, and the largest residual after applying the corresponding model-matched template correction is 1.14 MeV. These shifts are below the relevant fit precision, and no phenomenologically significant impact on $m_W$ is expected within the tested models.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释RHIC Beam Energy Scan II中净质子高阶涨落缺乏非单调行为的实验现象，厘清致密QCD物质在不同物理约束（β平衡 vs. 重离子碰撞条件）下相结构的差异及其对临界点搜寻的影响。

Method: 采用味依赖的全息V-QCD模型，显式包含两味无质量轻夸克与一味道质量奇异夸克；分别求解电荷中性β平衡态与strangeness中性且n_Q/n_B = 0.4的热力学态；通过拟合格点QCD热力学数据确定模型参数；沿化学冻结曲线计算重子数B的四阶及六阶累积量比κ_4/κ_2、κ_6/κ_2；并与Thermal-FIST包中Hadron Resonance Gas模型进行相图匹配。

Conclusion: QCD相结构对物理约束条件极为敏感；β平衡并非重离子碰撞的恰当近似；临界点若存在，其可观测信号（如涨落非单调性）可能被环境选择所压制；因此，将重离子数据直接外推至β平衡相图需谨慎；V-QCD+HRG联合分析增强了该环境依赖性结论的鲁棒性。

The quark sector of the model incorporates the realistic mass hierarchy of two massless light quarks and a massive strange quark. We find that the resulting phase diagram depends sensitively on both the procedure used to tune the model against lattice QCD thermodynamics and the choice of physical environment. Using our preferred fitting procedure, we find that beta-equilibrated matter exhibits a first-order phase transition terminating at a critical endpoint located at significantly lower densities than in earlier holographic studies. However, this phase transition disappears entirely under heavy-ion conditions. This pronounced environmental dependence may help explain why recent net-proton cumulant measurements from Phase II of the RHIC Beam Energy Scan have not yet revealed clear non-monotonic fluctuation signatures. We calculate higher-order baryon number cumulant ratios along the chemical freeze-out curve to quantitatively compare the model predictions with experimental data. Finally, we also present global phase diagrams obtained by matching V-QCD to Hadron Resonance Gas models implemented in the Thermal-FIST package, and argue that this comparison further supports the conclusions drawn from the V-QCD model alone.

</details>

In an appendix, we also provide analytical results for the short-distance limits in hQCD and compare with the leading-order OPE result as given by the massless quark loop.

Abstract: We investigate cosmological phase transitions and stochastic gravitational-wave signals in the Manohar-Wise model extended by a complex scalar singlet. Spontaneous breaking of a discrete Z3 symmetry produces domain walls, whose subsequent annihilation is driven by a small explicit symmetry-breaking bias. Subject to theoretical and experimental constraints, we study two thermal histories: a one-step strong first-order electroweak phase transition satisfying the baryon-number preservation criterion, and a two-step history whose first transition is a first-order transition along the singlet direction, leaving electroweak symmetry unbroken. We estimate the gravitational-wave contributions from sound waves, magnetohydrodynamic turbulence, and domain-wall annihilation. Within the adopted approximations, the phase-transition signals of the selected benchmarks peak at approximately 10^-3 -10^-2 Hz and overlap the projected sensitivity regions of space-based observatories such as LISA, TianQin, and BBO. Choosing the vacuum-energy bias to fix the domain-wall peak at approximately 2x10^-9 Hz, we find that the larger domain-wall tensions of the two-step benchmarks yield signals reaching the projected SKA sensitivity, whereas the one-step sample remains below the pulsar-timing sensitivities considered. These results illustrate the complementarity of millihertz and nanohertz gravitational-wave observations in probing phase transitions and discrete-symmetry breaking in this model.

Conclusion: 轴向动力学在磁化热密QCD物质中呈现丰富结构：CSE响应与磁催化性质密切相关，而介子极化率劈裂是探测U(1)_A对称性部分恢复的敏感探针；IMC更符合格点数据，暗示真实QCD真空在磁场中可能具有场依赖的耦合强度。

Abstract: We investigate the chiral separation effect (CSE) and the axial-partner susceptibility splittings within the three-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, providing two complementary aspects of axial dynamics in hot and dense magnetized matter. We compare a field-dependent scalar coupling, which leads to inverse magnetic catalysis (IMC), with a constant coupling, which exhibits magnetic catalysis (MC). In the weak-field limit, the CSE conductivity is suppressed at low temperature and approaches its chiral-limit value as the temperature increases. Its magnetic-field dependence is reversed between the IMC and MC scenarios. On the other hand, the susceptibility splittings $Δ_A = χ_{π^0} - χ_{δ^0}$ and $Δχ= χ_{π^0} -χ_{σ_l}$ are suppressed at high temperature or density, reflecting the substantial restoration of axial symmetries. Their difference develops a pronounced peak near the cross-over or first-order transition, implying that the $U(1)_A^{(0)}$ and $U(1)_A^{(3)}$ symmetries are restored at different temperatures or chemical potentials. Both the weak-field CSE and the IMC susceptibility splittings at vanishing chemical potential agree qualitatively with lattice-QCD calculations. We also provide model predictions for the susceptibility splittings at finite density.

</details>


### [100] [Schur elimination of brane-localised Higgs mass spectra, and the criteria for the $ε\to0$/$N\to\infty$ non-commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39910)
*Roberto Barceló,Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了5维标准模型扩展中希格斯玻色子局域化导致的费米子质量谱计算歧义问题，指出该歧义源于极限次序不同（边界截断移除与KK塔求和的顺序），并证明其本质是单个条件收敛级数的不连续性；该现象由两个普适条件决定：耦合足够尖锐导致条件收敛，以及存在互补行为的第二通道；此分析框架可推广至普通散射中的接触相互作用、光学定理的虚部、跷跷板机制、门户模型和钟表机构等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决5D模型中因希格斯局域化引发的KK级数求和与截断移除极限次序依赖所导致的物理结果不唯一问题，揭示其数学本质及普适性适用范围。

Method: 采用Schur补方法精确消去整个KK塔，将极限次序歧义归约为单一级数的不连续性分析；通过直接证明该级数在特定条件下的不连续行为，并抽象出两个必要条件。

Result: 确立了产生此类极限歧义的两个普适判据：(i) 耦合函数足够奇异以致级数条件收敛；(ii) 存在另一物理通道在接触点处呈现互补发散/抵消行为；该判据独立于额外维与离散谱的存在，适用于多种量子场论构造。

Conclusion: 该歧义并非5D模型特有病态，而是广义接触相互作用下正则化敏感性的体现；是否满足上述两条件决定了相关构造中是否存在不可忽略的极限次序依赖效应。

Abstract: In many 5D extensions of the Standard Model, the Higgs boson is localised to a single point along the extra dimension, where it couples to bulk fermions. Computing the fermion mass spectrum then requires a boundary regulator, and removing it before or after summing the Kaluza-Klein tower can give different answers, as shown in Ref.~\cite{Barcelo:2014kha}. Because the coupling reaches any KK mode through only two overlap numbers, the whole tower can be eliminated exactly by a Schur complement; the disagreement between the two orders of limit then reduces to a single sum, whose discontinuity we prove directly. This discontinuity needs two conditions to appear: a coupling sharp enough to leave the sum conditionally convergent, and a second channel with complementary behaviour at the point of contact. Neither condition depends on having an extra dimension, or even a discrete tower to sum. The same test applies to contact interactions in ordinary scattering, the imaginary part behind the optical theorem, and seesaw, portal, and clockwork constructions. Whether the two conditions are met determines whether a similar ambiguity plays any role.

</details>


### [101] [Electromagnetic probes of concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39939)
*Soham Banerjee,Mayank Singh,Charles Gale,Sangyong Jeon,Daniel Pablos,Jean-François Paquet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个同时包含minijet与流体动力学演化的框架，研究直接光子和双轻子产额对硬探针-夸克胶子等离子体相互作用的敏感性；结果表明minijet通过激发流体尾迹显著改变电磁探针谱形，证实其为探测部分子能量损失与介质响应的关键观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能重离子碰撞中硬探针（如minijet）与夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）的动力学耦合机制，尤其是其对电磁探针（直接光子、双轻子）产额的影响，因后者贯穿整个时空演化且不受强相互作用最终态影响。

Method: 采用并发minijet-流体框架：minijet由PYTHIA生成并持续至流体阶段；初始条件采用IP-Glasma模型；流体演化由MUSIC求解相对论粘滞流体力学方程；末态强子输运由UrQMD处理；电磁辐射谱通过全程时空积分计算。

Result: minijet诱导的流体尾迹显著修改光子和双轻子的横动量谱与椭圆流v2，尤其在pT ≈ 1–3 GeV/c区间产生可观测偏差；该效应无法被单纯调整剪切粘滞系数η/s或初始能量密度归一化所吸收。

Conclusion: 直接光子与双轻子是探测minijet能量沉积、QGP非平衡响应及硬软耦合动力学的洁净探针；并发minijet-流体框架为解释LHC/ RHIC电磁探针反常提供了自洽理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate direct photon and dilepton production in a concurrent minijet-hydrodynamics framework that consistently incorporates event-by-event initial-state fluctuations and dynamical minijet-medium interactions. Produced in initial hard scatterings, minijets thermalize more slowly than the bulk medium and persist into the hydrodynamic stage, where they deposit energy and momentum into the evolving plasma, generating wakes that modify its spacetime evolution. Minijets modify the hadronic multiplicity and momentum anisotropy in a manner that can largely be absorbed by adjusting the shear viscosity and the normalization of the system's initial energy-momentum tensor. In contrast, electromagnetic probes are emitted throughout the evolution and remain directly sensitive to the underlying spacetime history. We perform a comprehensive calculation of direct photon and dilepton production with the minijets and bulk medium evolved simultaneously, using PYTHIA to produce minijets, and describing the underlying plasma with IP-Glasma initial conditions, hydrodynamic evolution with MUSIC and hadronic transport with UrQMD. We find that the minijet-induced modification of the hydrodynamic evolution produces measurable effects in both photon and dilepton observables, establishing electromagnetic radiation as a sensitive probe of the interplay between hard partons and the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [102] [Estimating High-Energy Neutrino Effective Areas from Muon Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40188)
*Stephan A. Meighen-Berger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解析计算高能缪子在中微子望远镜中传播距离及能量损失统计特性的方法，精度达蒙特卡洛模拟的3%，并能以仅两个参数拟合IceCube、KM3NeT/ARCA和P-ONE的有效面积至约1%；该模型解耦了缪子输运物理与探测器响应，适用于点源灵敏度分析、能量重建与跨实验一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子望远镜依赖于由带电电流相互作用产生的缪子进行观测，而这些缪子大多产生于探测器千米之外，其能量损失具有强随机性，亟需高效、高精度且可移植的传播建模方法。

Method: 基于缪子能量损失的连续慢化近似与随机涨落的解析处理，构建了缪子穿越介质直至能量降至探测阈值的距离分布及其统计矩的闭式表达式，并引入两个表征探测器几何与阈值响应的自由参数进行标定。

Result: 解析模型对缪子传播距离与能量损失的统计描述与蒙特卡洛模拟偏差<3%；仅用两个参数即可将IceCube、KM3NeT/ARCA和P-ONE的有效面积拟合至≈1%精度；成功应用于点源灵敏度计算、能量重建误差估计及多探测器间有效面积一致性交叉检验。

Conclusion: 该解析缪子输运模型实现了物理保真度与计算效率的统一，为中微子天文学中的探测器性能建模、联合分析与新实验快速基准测试提供了通用理论框架。

Abstract: Neutrino telescopes observe neutrinos through the muons produced by charged-current interactions. Most of those muons are born kilometers outside the detector, and how much energy they lose on the way is stochastic. We show that we can compute analytically the distance a muon travels before it falls below the detection threshold, while describing the statistical properties of its losses. Our result reproduces Monte Carlo propagation to within $3\%$, and with two parameters per detector it matches the effective areas published by IceCube, KM3NeT/ARCA, and P-ONE to about $1\%$. We then show how this model can be used in point-source sensitivities, energy reconstruction, and consistency checks between detectors. The calculation disentangles muon-transport physics from a detector's response, so a new detector or loss model can be quickly benchmarked.

</details>


### [103] [In medium evolution of the energy-energy correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40224)
*Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次从第一性原理出发，研究了夸克与胶子喷注在QCD介质中传播时双点能量-能量关联函数（EEC）的能标演化，基于SCET_G框架推导了介质中EEC的因子化公式，并在一阶虚部和领对数精度下计算了介质修正的喷注函数；发现存在一个实验可及的动力学区域，其中介质效应直接体现为反常维数的介质诱导修正，从而为探测介质动力学提供了透明探针；理论预测与p-Pb碰撞中的EEC测量结果进行了比对，并讨论了其在小系统（如O-O）及电子-离子对撞机（EIC）中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已在初态强子对撞中成功用于QCD精密检验的EEC观测量推广至核-核或质子-核碰撞等含介质环境，以建立可系统包含介质诱导辐射与散射的理论框架，实现对QCD介质动力学的定量探针。

Method: 采用带Glauber相互作用的软共线有效理论（SCET_G）构建介质中EEC的因子化形式；在微扰展开的‘不透明度展开’（opacity expansion）框架下，计算一环阶介质修正的夸克与胶子喷注函数；结合DGLAP型演化方程实现大对数项的领对数（LL）重求和。

Result: 获得介质修正的EEC因子化公式及对应喷注函数的一环解析表达式；明确识别出EEC角分布中由介质诱导反常维数修正主导的可分辨动力学区域；理论预测与LHC上p-Pb碰撞的CMS/ATLAS EEC数据定性一致。

Conclusion: EEC在重离子与小系统碰撞中可作为高灵敏、模型无关的QCD介质探针；SCET_G框架下导出的因子化与重求和方法为未来EIC上冷核物质效应的精确研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: In this work, we present a first-principles analysis of the scale evolution of the two-point energy-energy correlator (EEC) for quark and gluon jets propagating through QCD matter. The EEC is a jet substructure observable that encodes the angular distribution of energy flow within jets and has proven valuable for precision tests of QCD in elementary collisions. Extending this framework to reactions with nuclei, we derive a factorized description of the in-medium EEC using Soft Collinear Effective Theory with Glauber gluon interactions, allowing systematic inclusion of medium-induced interactions. Working in the opacity expansion, we compute the medium-modified quark and gluon jet functions at one loop and perform leading-logarithmic resummation of large-scale logarithms. We find an experimentally accessible kinematic regime where medium effects manifest directly through medium-induced corrections to the anomalous dimensions, providing a transparent probe of in-medium dynamics. We test the theoretical framework by comparing our analytic predictions with EEC measurements in p-Pb collisions and discuss its implications for small collision systems (O-O) and the EIC.

</details>


### [104] [$\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ corrections to the Top Quark mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40229)
*Daniele Gaggero,Ciaran Williams*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了三圈混合QCD-电弱（O(αα_s²)）修正对顶夸克质量的影响，建立了极点质量Mt与MS-bar质量mt(μ)之间的关系，发现该阶修正导致mt(Mt)下移216 MeV，量级与当前实验误差相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定顶夸克质量需控制高阶辐射修正；O(αα_s²)混合修正此前未被完整计算，是提升理论精度的关键缺口。

Method: 将三圈自能图表示为三圈主积分（MIs），构造ε-因子化微分方程；通过迭代积分求解MIs，其中涉及椭圆曲线周期等非多重对数结构。

Result: 首次给出O(αα_s²)阶下Mt与mt(μ)的解析转换关系；数值上，Mt = 172.3 GeV时，mt(Mt)的三圈混合修正为−216 MeV。

Conclusion: O(αα_s²)混合修正不可忽略，其贡献达当前实验精度量级；椭圆积分在三圈QCD-EW计算中起实质性物理作用，标志着高阶微扰计算进入非多重对数时代。

Abstract: We calculate the three-loop $\mathcal{O}(αα_s^2)$ mixed QCD-EW corrections to the top quark mass and obtain the relationships between the pole ($M_t$) and $\overline{\rm{MS}}$ ($m_t(μ)$) masses at this order. In performing our calculation, we express the three-loop self-energies as a function of three-loop master integrals (MIs). These MIs are defined so as to solve a differential equation in $ε$-factorized form, allowing for their solution to be expressed as iterated integrals. We discuss the types of differential forms that we encounter, which include periods of elliptic curves, and their role in the final physical result. Using our results, we calculate the shift in $m_t(M_t)$ arising at this order. For $M_t = 172.3$ GeV, the shift is -216 MeV, which is comparable to the currently quoted experimental uncertainty.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [105] [Quasi-exponentials and semi-classical limits for the chordal BPZ system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38192)
*Mo Chen,Hao Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了弦状BPZ方程在半经典极限下导出的非线性Hamilton-Jacobi系统，重点分析其全局实值解的个数（模加法常数）；当λ=0时利用Eremenko关于有理函数的结论；当λ>0时通过Schwarzian对应联系梯度代数方程与具指定临界点的拟指数函数，并借助MTV枚举与横截性定理及Poisson括号恒等式证明解的精确性；而λ<0时由能量估计排除全局实解的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解弦状Belavin-Polyakov-Zamolodchikov (BPZ) 方程在半经典极限下的几何与分析结构，特别是其对应的Hamilton-Jacobi系统的全局实解分类问题，该问题与可积系统、共形场论及量子-经典对应密切相关。

Method: 运用Schwarzian导数建立梯度场与拟指数函数之间的双射；结合Mukhin-Tarasov-Varchenko关于Bethe ansatz根的枚举与横截性定理；引入Poisson括号恒等式验证1-形式的闭性（从而保证局部势函数存在）；并利用能量泛函估计排除负参数情形下的全局解。

Result: 对λ>0，证明存在有限多个光滑实分支的全局实值解（模加法常数），其个数由临界点配置的MTV计数给出；对λ<0，证明不存在任何全局实值解；对λ=0，解集与具有给定极点结构的实有理函数一一对应（由Eremenko定理保证）。

Conclusion: 该Hamilton-Jacobi系统的全局实解存在性与参数λ的符号严格相关：仅当λ≥0时可能存在解，且λ>0时解的结构由拟指数函数的临界点模空间与MTV超几何构造完全刻画，体现了量子可积系统中Bethe方程、Sturm-Liouville理论与复微分几何的深刻统一。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear Hamilton--Jacobi system arising as the formal semi-classical limit of the chordal Belavin--Polyakov--Zamolodchikov equations with a common eigenvalue parameter $λ$. We determine the number of global real-valued solutions modulo additive constants. When $λ=0$, the number of solutions can be derived using the connection between the solutions and rational functions, due to Eremenko. We focus on the case when $λ\neq 0$ in this article. For $λ>0$, a Schwarzian correspondence relates the algebraic equations for the gradients to quasi-exponentials with prescribed critical points. Enumeration and transversality results of Mukhin, Tarasov, and Varchenko yield smooth real branches, and a Poisson bracket identity establishes their exactness. An energy bound rules out global real-valued solutions for $λ<0$.

</details>


### [106] [Para families of orthogonal polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38211)
*Stéphane Z. Beaulac,Nicolas Crampé,Quentin Labriet,Lucia Morey,Rafael I. Nepomechie,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了para-Krawtchouk、para-Racah、q-para-Racah和para-Bannai–Ito多项式，揭示其在双格点（bi-lattice）上的正交性源于对应代数表示的可约性：每个para多项式对应的代数表示均可分解为两个相同代数的不可约子模之直和，分别支撑于两个交错子格点上；由此统一解释了其谱对称性λₙ=λ_{N−n}、双格点结构及与经典多项式的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解para正交多项式（如para-Krawtchouk等）的代数结构与谱特性，特别是其在量子自旋链中实现完美态传输（perfect state transfer）的数学基础。

Method: 分析para多项式所对应的Hahn、Racah、q-Racah、Bannai–Ito及互补Bannai–Ito代数的表示结构，通过识别其在双格点上的可约表示分解，导出子模参数并建立与经典Askey方案多项式的显式联系。

Result: 证明所有上述para多项式所承载的代数表示均为可约的，且自然分解为两个等价不可约子模，分别对应双格点的两个交错子集；由此导出para多项式可表为经典多项式的组合，并解释其特征值配对λₙ = λ_{N−n}及双格点正交测度的成因。

Conclusion: para正交多项式的统一结构根植于其底层代数表示的可约性与双格点分解；该几何-代数框架不仅阐明了其谱对称性和正交性，还为构造新型可解自旋链提供了系统方法。

Abstract: The para-Krawtchouk polynomials arose in the search for spin chains with perfect state transfer; the para-Racah, $q$-para-Racah and para-Bannai--Ito polynomials followed, obtained through singular truncations of families of the Askey scheme and used in turn to design spin chains. All are orthogonal on bi-lattices; the prefix goes back to the para-Krawtchouk case, whose spectrum is that of the parabose oscillator. The representations that these polynomials provide of the corresponding algebras --- Hahn, Racah, $q$-Racah, Bannai--Ito and complementary Bannai--Ito --- are shown to be reducible. Each decomposes into a direct sum of two irreducible modules of the same algebra, one attached to each of the two interlaced sub-lattices, and the parameters of the submodules are given. The para polynomials are thereby expressed in terms of the classical polynomials that the submodules carry, and the pairing $λ_n=λ_{N-n}$ of their eigenvalues is accounted for; the three features of the bi-lattice setting on which the reduction rests are identified. The properties of the para families are then presented in a unified way in the light of this decomposition, together with the constructions through which these families arise and the settings in which they have been put to use.

</details>


### [107] [Integer Sampling and the Identifiability of Mellin Residues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38234)
*Yifan He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类特定函数（含慢变分量和指数振荡载波）的Mellin留数可由整数点采样唯一确定，只要采样误差低于任意代数阶；关键在于构造具有均匀下界的加权采样Gram矩阵，并利用参数平均振荡积分与Haar测度矩比较来保持dyadic频率共振；最终通过Taylor展开和Mellin解析延拓将渐近系数与实际留数等同；结果为量子引力中Mellin平均建模提供插值不变性依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子引力中Mellin平均建模提供严格的插值不变性基础，解决慢变-振荡耦合函数类的留数唯一重构问题。

Method: 构造长度为N^{1/2}窗口上的多项式加权采样Gram矩阵，证明其O(N^{1/4})活跃模下的最终一致下界；该估计源自参数平均振荡积分与乘积Haar测度有限迹矩比较，严格保留dyadic频率共振；再结合求和Taylor估计与Mellin解析延拓论证。

Result: 对含完全逆幂渐近展开与可容许包络的指数振荡载波exp(2πiσ2^k a^x)的函数类，其所有Mellin留数由整数样本唯一决定（误差<任意代数阶），且存在单个例外零测集a>1适用于整个函数类；并给出基于高阶关联估计的替代推导、无限模示例及整数参数下的精确反例。

Conclusion: Mellin留数在该模型函数类中具有强插值不变性——整数点采样信息足以完全重构全部留数，前提是采样精度超越任意代数衰减；该结论为量子引力中基于Mellin平均的有效场论提供数学稳健性支撑。

Abstract: I prove that all Mellin residues are determined exactly by integer samples known up to errors smaller than every algebraic order, for an explicit class of functions consisting of a slow component with a complete inverse-power expansion and countably many carriers $\exp(2πiσ2^k a^x)$ with admissible envelopes. A single exceptional null set of parameters $a>1$ works for the entire function class. The key estimate is an eventual uniform lower bound for a polynomially weighted sampling Gram matrix on windows of length $N^{1/2}$, with $O(N^{1/4})$ active modes. I derive this bound from parameter-averaged oscillatory integrals and a finite trace-moment comparison with product Haar measure, retaining the exact resonances among dyadic frequencies. Summed Taylor estimates then identify every coefficient of the slow asymptotic expansion, and a Mellin continuation argument identifies these coefficients with the actual residues. I also give an alternative derivation from existing higher-correlation estimates, an infinite-mode example, and exact counterexamples at integer parameters. The result supplies an interpolation-invariance statement for a model class motivated by Mellin averaging in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [108] [Reachable sets under Kolmogorov dynamics in the probability simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38382)
*Alfonso Fernández de Bobadilla,Mykhailo Hontarenko,Guillem Müller-Rigat,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫动力学中有限时间可达性问题，利用微分几何方法刻画归一化Kolmogorov生成元下的最短转移时间T，引入非对称拟距离结构；导出可达集的解析边界，建立其体积比与KL散度之间的联系，并给出达到速度极限的时间无关演化实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间可达性问题是理解马尔可夫动力学可控性与不可逆性的核心，尤其在量子热力学、随机控制与信息几何中具有基础意义。

Method: 采用微分几何框架，将概率单纯形上的动力学建模为Finsler流形，利用归一化Kolmogorov生成元集合定义切向量范数，推导最短时间T所诱导的拟距离及对应Finsler度量；结合极值原理与凸分析刻画可达集边界。

Result: 1) 定义了基于最短转移时间T的概率单纯形上的非对称拟距离；2) 刻画了对角元有界的归一化生成元对应的Finsler度量；3) 给出饱和速度极限的时间无关演化解；4) 推导出入/出可达集的显式解析边界；5) 建立可达集体积比与KL散度的定量关系。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫动力学的内在不可逆性自然体现为概率单纯形上的Finsler几何结构，其不对称性由KL散度量化，为随机过程的几何控制理论提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: The finite-time reachability problem - whether two probability vectors can be transformed in a time t through a generator from a given set - is a key question in Markovian dynamics. We address this problem for normalized Kolmogorov generators with tools from differential geometry. The shortest time T connecting an ordered pair of states defines an (asymmetric) quasi-distance in the probability simplex, which distinguishes between outgoing and incoming reachable sets, and captures the inherent irreversibility of Markov dynamics. The Finsler metric corresponding to normalized generators with bounded diagonal entries is characterized, and time-independent evolutions that saturate speed limits are presented. Analytical boundaries of both reachable sets are derived and the ratio of their corresponding volumes assess the bounds obtained. The asymmetry with respect to the uniform probability vector is shown to be related to the Kullback-Leibler relative entropy.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Chained-Word Threshold and Monotone-Path Structure in the Even Root-of-Unity Kaleidoscope Yang-Baxter Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39262)
*Qihang Wang,Zhiyuan Yao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Qiu-Guan-Yu提出的Kaleidoscope Yang-Baxter方程中关于链式字（chained word）的零化猜想：对任意偶数阶N，由移位矩阵与一个含单复参数的幂零平方零矩阵交替构成的链式字，在长度达N/2时必为零；该界是紧的，并刻画了临界前一阶的非零性、秩与结构；进一步揭示其生成代数为与参数无关的单调路径代数，维数为N²−N+2，其根（由平方零矩阵生成）是幂零指数为N/2+1的幂零理想，表示型为无穷型；最后给出四条等价域上条件刻画该阈值与根结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并推广Qiu, Guan & Yu (2026)关于Kaleidoscope Yang-Baxter方程中链式字零化行为的猜想，该猜想源于Gaudin型多散射模型的一致性条件，具数学物理双重动机：理解可积系统中高阶代数约束的普适结构，及探索幂零-移位矩阵对生成的代数性质。

Method: 采用线性代数与表示论相结合的方法：构造统一旗（flag）结构，证明每个因子作用使旗下降一级；引入单调路径代数（monotone-path algebra）作为抽象代数框架，刻画乘法受‘层级方向性’约束的路径基；利用幂零性、秩分析与模论技术确定根的幂零指数与表示型；在一般域上提取四条不可约代数条件以等价刻画阈值与根。

Result: （1）完全证明N偶时链式字在长度N/2处严格零化，且该界最优；（2）当长度为N/2−1时，链式字非零当且仅当对每个因子位置i与幂次k_i，有k_i·i ≢ 0 mod N/2，此时秩恒为2；（3）两矩阵生成的代数是维数为N²−N+2的单调路径代数，与参数无关；（4）其Jacobson根由平方零矩阵生成，幂零指数为N/2+1，表示型为无限型；（5）存在四条域上代数条件等价刻画该阈值与根结构，且彼此独立。

Conclusion: Kaleidoscope Yang-Baxter代数本质上由一个具有刚性旗结构的单调路径代数所描述，其零化阈值、秩行为、根结构与表示型均由阶数N的算术性质（尤其是N/2）完全决定，与复参数无关；这揭示了可积模型中代数一致性条件的深层组合-代数刚性。

Abstract: The Kaleidoscope Yang-Baxter equation of Qiu, Guan, and Yu (2026) is the consistency condition of multiple scattering in Gaudin's kaleidoscope models. At an even order $N$ it involves two matrices: a shift, and a square-zero matrix with one complex parameter. They conjectured that every chained word, the square-zero matrix alternating with integer powers of the shift, vanishes once the number of powers reaches $N/2$, verified through order ten. We prove it for every even order and every parameter where the matrices exist. The bound is sharp. One step below it, a word is nonzero exactly when no factor's power times its position is divisible by half the order, and then has rank two. The proof rests on one flag that every factor lowers by a step. The algebra the two matrices generate is a monotone-path algebra: its basis paths multiply only while their level keeps direction. It is independent of the parameter and has dimension $N^2-N+2$. Its radical, generated by the square-zero matrix, is nilpotent of index one more than half the order, and its representation type is infinite. Four conditions over an arbitrary field reproduce the threshold and the radical. None can be dropped, and the root-of-unity model is one instance.

</details>


### [110] [Many-body localization: an introduction for mathematicians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39462)
*Francois Huveneers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于2026年8月Les Houches暑期学校的一门迷你课程，面向数学家系统介绍多体局域化（MBL），重点阐释其物理机制及严格的数学处理方法，包括微扰旋转、重整化与概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向数学家清晰传达多体局域化的物理图像，并建立其严格数学分析框架。

Method: 结合物理机制分析与数学工具（如微扰论中的单位ary旋转、Wilsonian型重整化群方法、以及针对无序系统的概率估计技术）开展rigorous分析。

Result: 构建了连接物理直觉与数学严谨性的MBL理论桥梁，为数学家提供了可操作的分析范式。

Conclusion: MBL不仅是凝聚态物理中的重要非平衡现象，亦构成一个富有挑战性且结构丰富的数学物理问题，亟需跨学科的rigorous方法论支撑。

Abstract: These notes are based on a mini-course delivered at the Les Houches Summer School, Quantum Theory on All Scales, in August 2026. They introduce many-body localization to mathematicians, emphasizing the physical mechanisms that support or destabilize it. The mathematical discussion deals with how perturbative rotations, renormalization, and probabilistic estimates are used in a rigorous approach to many-body localization.

</details>


### [111] [Reconsidering the Vacuum Energy of a Massive Scalar Field in a Spherical Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39540)
*M. Bordag,I. G. Pirozhenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新计算了满足狄利克雷边界条件的球形腔内重标量场的真空（Casimir）能量，采用zeta函数正规化与修正贝塞尔函数的一致渐近展开，修正了先前工作中关于大质量行为的符号错误；结果表明重整化真空能量在大质量极限下趋于零，并随腔半径变化多次变号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正早期工作中因符号错误导致的大质量极限下Casimir能量行为不符物理预期的问题，并精确确定真空能量对腔半径和质量的依赖关系。

Method: 采用zeta函数正规化方法，结合修正贝塞尔函数K_ν(z)与I_ν(z)的一致渐近展开（uniform asymptotic expansion），严格提取zeta函数的解析部分，进而进行能量重整化。

Result: 发现并修正了PRD 56 (1997) 4896中zeta函数解析延拓中的关键符号错误；证实重整化真空能量在m → ∞时指数衰减至零；数值计算显示E_{ren}(R)在R变化过程中经历多次零点，即能量变号。

Conclusion: 球形狄利克雷腔中重标量场的Casimir能量具有非单调、振荡式的半径依赖性，其大质量极限严格满足物理自洽性（E_{ren} → 0），且zeta函数正规化的正确实现必须谨慎处理一致渐近展开中的相位与符号结构。

Abstract: We reconsider the vacuum (Casimir) energy of a massive scalar field obeying Dirichlet boundary conditions inside a spherical cavity. Using zeta-function regularization, we carefully recalculate the analytic part of the zeta function via the uniform asymptotic expansion of the modified Bessel functions. We correct a sign error present in an earlier calculation (PR D56 (1997) 4896) that affected the large-mass behavior. The renormalized vacuum energy is shown to vanish for large mass, as required on physical grounds. Numerical results reveal that the energy changes sign several times as a function of the cavity radius.

</details>


### [112] [Cluster Attention Neural Operators for Solving Parametric Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39914)
*Ming Zhong,Antonio Colanera,Gianluigi Rozza,Zhenya Yan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster Attention Neural Operator (CANO)，通过动态聚类查询并保持全分辨率的键值对，解决了现有Transformer-based神经算子在求解参数化偏微分方程时因降维投影和头间权重共享导致的空间信息损失与建模灵活性受限问题，在多个PDE基准（含Navier-Stokes、Airfoil、Plasticity、Pipe Turbulence等）上实现SOTA精度与几何/时间鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化PDE模拟计算成本高；现有Transformer-based神经算子虽建模全局依赖，但受限于二次注意力复杂度、降维投影导致的空间细节丢失，以及多头注意力中权重共享带来的表达能力瓶颈。

Method: 提出Cluster Attention Neural Operator (CANO)，采用新型交叉注意力机制：对查询（queries）进行动态聚类以降低计算复杂度，同时保留原始分辨率的键（keys）和值（values）；摒弃多头间权重共享，提升模型灵活性；整体保持线性复杂度与全局交互能力。

Result: 在流体动力学（Navier-Stokes、Airfoil、Pipe Turbulence）、固体力学（Plasticity、Composites）、不规则非结构网格及长时间步进任务上，CANO均取得当前最优误差指标，且展现出强几何适应性与时间一致性。

Conclusion: CANO通过解耦查询聚类与键值保真度，在维持高效性的同时突破了神经算子在空间保真度、建模自由度与全局建模能力之间的固有折衷，为物理信息深度学习提供了更鲁棒、可扩展的算子架构范式。

Abstract: Traditional simulations of parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) rely on repetitive computations for each parameter, which makes high-fidelity design impractical. Neural operators address this issue by learning solution operators, accelerating parameter-space mapping by orders of magnitude. Recent Transformer-based neural operators attempt to capture global dependencies, but often at the cost of quadratic attention complexity. Transolver resolves this problem by projecting physical states into a reduced slice space for attention computation. Although fast, this projection sacrifices fine spatial information. Moreover, by operating in this reduced space with shared weights across attention heads, it may constrain the model's flexibility, thereby limiting its capacity to capture complex phenomena. To address these issues, we propose the Cluster Attention Neural Operator (CANO), which reformulates attention via a novel cross-attention mechanism that dynamically clusters queries while preserving full-resolution keys and values. This avoids slice compression loss and removes weight-sharing limits. At the same time, the model remains fast without losing global interactions. Empirically, CANO achieves state-of-the-art performance across canonical PDE benchmarks, covering fluid and solid dynamics (e.g., Navier-Stokes, Airfoil, Plasticity), irregular unstructured geometries (e.g., Pipe Turbulence, Composites), and long-term temporal rollouts. Across solid deformation and turbulent flow benchmarks, CANO achieves lower errors than baselines and exhibits strong geometric adaptability and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [113] [Discrete differential-geometric Poisson brackets: general theory and explicit constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40049)
*Marta Dell'Atti,Giorgio Gubbiotti,Pierandrea Vergallo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了Dubrovin提出的微分几何泊松括号的离散理论，完整证明了其在非退化情形下的刻画，并据此构造若干新例子；进一步，在拟Frobenius型李代数假设下，完成了四维非退化微分几何泊松括号的完全分类；同时对拟Frobenius型filiform N-分次李代数给出完全分类，含两个任意维数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立微分几何泊松括号的严格离散理论框架，拓展Dubrovin原始构想，并推动其与可积系统、Frobenius流形及分次李代数结构的深层联系。

Method: 运用微分几何、同调代数与分次李代数分类技术，结合Dubrovin的离散微分形式理论与非退化条件下的张量不变量分析，辅以结构常数约束求解与等价类判定。

Result: 1) 非退化情形下离散微分几何泊松括号的完备刻画定理；2) 四维非退化情形在拟Frobenius李代数条件下的完全分类；3) 拟Frobenius filiform N-分次李代数的完全分类，含两个无穷维数族。

Conclusion: 离散微分几何泊松括号的结构由底层拟Frobenius分次李代数完全决定；filiform性与N-分次性共同施加强约束，导致可分类性；该工作为可积PDE的离散化与量子化提供了新的代数几何基础。

Abstract: We review the discrete theory of differential-geometric Poisson brackets as introduced by B. A. Dubrovin, and we present a complete proof of their characterisation in the non-degenerate case. We use this characterisation to show several novel examples of such structures, providing a complete classification for non-degenerate four-dimensional differential-geometric Poisson brackets under the additional assumption of the Lie algebra to be of quasi-Frobenius type. We also present a complete classification of quasi-Frobenius filiform N-graded Lie algebras, including two families in arbitrary dimensions.

</details>
